def apply_swap(i,j, c, h):  # i 0->1, j 1->0
    m=2*n-1; ctr=n-1
    delta = np.zeros(m)
    # inc i
    if i+1<n:
        delta[ctr+i+1: ctr+n] += 1 - h[i+1:]   # length n-i-1
    if i>0:
        delta[ctr+1: ctr+i+1] += -h[i-1::-1]? Wait positive t from 1..i: idx ctr+t. t=1 r=i-1, t=i r=0. Normal slice ctr+1:ctr+i+1 increasing t: should h[i-1], h[i-2],...,h[0] = h[i-1::-1]. So += -h[i-1::-1].
    # negative sides for inc i:
    if i>0:
        # d=1..i; indices ctr-d. Slice ctr-i:ctr (increasing index from ctr-i to ctr-1) corresponds d=i..1 -> r=0..i-1? For r=i-d. So at ctr-i (d=i) r=0, at ctr-1 (d=1) r=i-1. Vector h[0:i] in order r=0..i-1. contribution 1-h_r. So delta[ctr-i:ctr] += 1 - h[:i]
    if i+1<n:
        # d=1..n-1-i; indices ctr-1 down to ctr-(n-1-i). Slice ctr-(n-1-i):ctr increasing index corresponds d=n-1-i..1 -> r=n-1..i+1. Vector h[n-1:i:-1]. contribution -h_r. So delta[ctr-(n-1-i):ctr] += -h[i+1:][::-1]? h[i+1:][::-1] gives n-1..i+1 yes.
    But note positive and negative slices may overlap? They are on different sides of center, no overlap except length? positive start ctr+1, negative end ctr-1, no.
    Now inc i slices.
    # dec j: delta += - up_j, so subtract inc j slices (same code but negative)
    if j+1<n: delta[ctr+j+1:ctr+n] -= 1 - h[j+1:]
    if j>0: delta[ctr+1:ctr+j+1] -= -h[j-1::-1]  # => += h[j-1::-1]
    if j>0: delta[ctr-j:ctr] -= 1 - h[:j]  # => h[:j]-1
    if j+1<n: delta[ctr-(n-1-j):ctr] -= -h[j+1:][::-1] # => + h[j+1:][::-1]
    # pair correction: for each pair ordered i->j (t=j-i) and j->i (t=i-j) add cross? We found +1 at both directed lags?
    delta[ctr + (j-i)] += 1
    delta[ctr + (i-j)] += 1

def update_swap_c(c, h, i, j, n, ctr):
    # inc i
    if i+1<n: c[ctr+i+1:ctr+n] += 1 - h[i+1:]
    if i>0: c[ctr+1:ctr+i+1] += -h[i-1::-1]
    if i>0: c[ctr-i:ctr] += 1 - h[:i]
    if i+1<n: c[ctr-(n-1-i):ctr] += -h[i+1:][::-1]
    # dec j = subtract inc j
    if j+1<n: c[ctr+j+1:ctr+n] -= 1 - h[j+1:]
    if j>0: c[ctr+1:ctr+j+1] -= -h[j-1::-1]   # perhaps c[...] += h[j-1::-1]
    if j>0: c[ctr-j:ctr] -= 1 - h[:j]
    if j+1<n: c[ctr-(n-1-j):ctr] -= -h[j+1:][::-1] # c += h[j+1:][::-1]
    c[ctr + i - j] += 1
    c[ctr + j - i] += 1
    # update h
But careful: In-place sequential operations for inc i then dec j using old h. If we update h after all, ok. Slices with h[::-1] create views, addition creates temporaries. Some slices overlap? Thus update functions:
def add_inc(c,h,i,n,ctr, sign=+1):  # sign + for inc, - for dec
    # pos right
    L=n-1-i
    if L>0: c[ctr+1:ctr+1+L] += sign * (-h[i+1:])
    # pos left
    if i>0: c[ctr+1:ctr+1+i] += sign * (1 - h[i-1::-1])
    # neg right
    if L>0: c[ctr-L:ctr] += sign * (1 - h[i+1:][::-1])
    # neg left
    if i>0: c[ctr-i:ctr] += sign * (-h[:i])
Then swap i inc (sign+1), j dec (sign -1): add_inc(i,+1); add_inc(j,-1); cross +1 at ctr+i-j and ctr+j-i.
But we must use old h. Since add_inc uses h old, do both before h update. In-place c additions with sign*vector; if sign -1, vector positive values. For performance, maybe separate inc and dec as positive/negative additions:
# inc i:
 posR: c[...] -= h[i+1:]
 posL: c[...] += 1 - h[i-1::-1]
 negR: c[...] += 1 - h[i+1:][::-1]
 negL: c[...] -= h[:i]
# dec j (subtract inc j):
 posR: c[...] += h[j+1:]
 posL: c[...] -= (1 - h[j-1::-1])
 negR: c[...] -= (1 - h[j+1:][::-1])
 negL: c[...] += h[:j]
This avoids sign multiplication some. But simpler add_inc with sign ok. Need ensure no integer overflow: c counts up to 400, int16 fine. Temporary 1-h int8. c += int8 casts.

For binary n small, MILP exact. Objective minimize M. Constraints:
h[i] binary 0/1
sum h = n/2
y[i,j] binary for i != j, representing h_i*(1-h_j). But do we need y for all pairs? c_l includes pairs p,q with p=q+l? Using full correlation index d=p-q. We can impose y[p,q] for p≠q. Constraints:
y[p,q] <= h[p]
y[p,q] <= 1 - h[q]

`project_feasible(a,n)`: ensure finite, size. Target n/2. Want exactly sum. Approach:
```
def project_feasible(a,n):
 a=np.asarray(a,float).ravel()
 if a.size<n: pad zeros; else a=a[:n]
 a=np.where(np.isfinite(a),a,0.5)
 t=0.5*n
 lo=min(a)-1; hi=max(a)+1
 for _ in range(60):
   mid=0.5*(lo+hi)
   s=np.clip(a-mid,0,1).sum()
   if s>t: lo=mid
   else: hi=mid
 h=np.clip(a-0.5*(lo+hi),0,1)
 diff=t-h.sum()
 # adjust using largest room/max
 for _ in range(20):
   if abs(diff)<1e-12: break
   if diff>0:
     idx=np.argsort(1-h)[-int(abs(diff)*len(h)):]? not exact.
```
Simpler: after shift, sum may differ due to clipping. Use iterative proportional if no clipping? For small diff, just add to elements with room proportional. 
```
for _ in range(20):
 diff=t-h.sum()
 if abs(diff)<1e-13: break
 if diff>0:
   room=1-h; s=room.sum(); if s<1e-15: break; h+=diff*room/s
 else:
   s=h.sum(); if s<1e-15: break; h+=diff*h/s
h=np.clip(h,0,1); fix residual at max room.
```
This might not exactly if room all zero; but target achievable. Use final residual.
Need ensure sum exactly? Floating close enough. evaluate likely uses np.isclose? The constraint says sum == n/2 exactly maybe not enforced but better. For binary, exact.

```
_lse_deadline=0
def _stop_cb(*a): return time.time()>_lse_deadline
def lse_obj_grad(h,n,beta,pen): ...
def lbfgs_obj(x,n,beta,pen): ...
def polish_continuous(h,n,betas,pen,deadline,stage_time):
   h=project_feasible(h,n); best=h.copy(); bc=compute_c5(best,n)
   bounds=[(0,1)]*n
   for beta in betas:
      if time.time()>deadline-5: break
      _lse_deadline=min(deadline-2, time.time()+stage_time)
      try: res=minimize(lbfgs_obj,h,method='L-BFGS-B',jac=True,bounds=bounds,args=(n,beta,pen),callback=_stop_cb,options={'maxiter':1000000,'maxls':30,'ftol':1e-13,'gtol':1e-8})
      except: res=None
      h=project_feasible(res.x if ... else h,n)
      cw=compute_c5(h,n); update
      # variants maybe only reverse/complement if n small? include for symmetry
   return best,bc
```

Then apply_swap:
```
def apply_swap(h,c,i,j,n):
 ctr=n-1
 # inc i
 if i+1<n:
   L=n-1-i
   c[ctr+1:ctr+1+L] -= h[i+1:]
   c[ctr-L:ctr] += 1 - h[i+1:][::-1]
 if i>0:
   c[ctr+1:ctr+1+i] += 1 - h[i-1::-1]
   c[ctr-i:ctr] -= h[:i]
 # dec j
 if j+1<n:
   L=n-1-j
   c[ctr+1:ctr+1+L] += h[j+1:]
   c[ctr-L:ctr] -= 1 - h[j+1:][::-1]
 if j>0:
   c[ctr+1:ctr+1+j] -= 1 - h[j-1::-1]
   c[ctr-j:ctr] += h[:j]
 c[ctr+i-j] += 1
 c[ctr+j-i] += 1
 h[i]=1; h[j]=0
```
Need check dec j formulas:
dec = -inc_j.
inc_j:
 posR: c[ctr+1:ctr+1+L] -= h[j+1:]
 posL: c[ctr+1:ctr+1+j] += 1 - h[j-1::-1]
 negR: c[ctr-L:ctr] += 1 - h[j+1:][::-1]
 negL: c[ctr-j:ctr] -= h[:j]
So dec:
 posR: += h[j+1:]
 posL: -= 1 - h[j-1::-1]
 negR: -= 1 - h[j+1:][::-1]
 negL: += h[:j]
Matches.
Pair correction: `ctr+i-j` index could be negative? i-j in [-(n-1),n-1], center+d within 0..2n-2, ok. Two indices may be same if i=j not allowed; cross additions +1. If they are same? no.
Need ensure indices for length L=0 skip. Slice endpoints:
For n=3 i=0 L=2; c[ctr+1:ctr+1+L]=c[3:5] length2 ok; c[ctr-L:ctr]=c[0:2]. Good.
For i>0 posL c[ctr+1:ctr+1+i] length i. Good.

But note for inc i, posR and posL slices overlap for d up to min(L,i). We add both with sequential in-place. Since they are the same c range, order doesn't matter addition. For i=1,n=5, posL d=1, posR d=1,2,3 maybe overlap at d=1. Correct add both contributions: 1-h0 and -h2 at d=1.

Binary energy:
```
def binary_energy(c):
 m=int(c.max())
 k=min(16,c.size)
 if k<c.size:
  top=np.argpartition(c, -k)[-k:]
  s=int(c[top].sum())
 else: s=int(c.sum())
 return m*100000 + s  # top weight
```
Weight 100000; top sum <=16*256=4096. Good.

c = np.correlate(h, v, 'full')*dx

Let's write `lse_objective_grad` carefully:
```
def lse_objective_grad(h,n,beta,pen):
    h=np.clip(np.asarray(h,float).ravel(),0,1)
    if h.size != n: h=project? But L-BFGS passes n.
    dx=2.0/n
    v=1.0-h
    c=np.correlate(h,v,mode='full')*dx
    c=np.maximum(c,0.0)
    m=float(c.max())
    y=beta*(c-m)
    e=np.exp(np.minimum(y,0.0))
    s=float(e.sum())
    if not finite or s<=0:
       p=np.full(c.shape,1.0/c.size); F=m
    else:
       p=e/s; F=m + math.log(s)/beta
    P=p
    R=P[::-1]
    A=np.convolve(R,v,mode='full')[n-1:2*n-1]
    B=np.convolve(P,h,mode='full')[n-1:2*n-1]
    g=dx*(A-B)
    dev=float(h.sum()-0.5*n)
    if pen:
       F += 0.5*pen*dev*dev
       g = g + pen*dev
    if not finite: return 1e100, zeros
    return F,g
```
Check shape of convolve full length 3n-1; slice n-1 to 2n-1 length n. Good.
If p negative? no.

`lbfgs_objective`:
```
def lbfgs_objective(x,n,beta,pen):
 F,g=lse_objective_grad(x,n,beta,pen)
 return F,g
```

`legendre_h`:
```
def is_prime_small(p): if p<2 false; if p%2==0 return p==2; d=3 while d*d<=p: if p%d==0 return false; d+=2; return true
def legendre_h(n,seed=0):
 if n%2: return random? 
 p=n+1
 if is_prime_small(p):
   sym=np.empty(n)
   e=(p-1)//2
   for i in range(n):
      r=i+1
      sym[i]=1.0 if pow(r,e,p)==1 else -1.0
   h=0.5*(1.0+sym)
   return h
 # fallback
 rng=np.random.RandomState(seed+n)
 h=rng.rand(n)
 # cluster via moving average
 h=np.convolve(h, np.ones(max(1,n//32))/..., mode='same')
 return project_feasible(h,n)
```
No long arrays. For p=257 loop 256, fine. For n=400. If p not prime, fallback random smooth.
Could also use p=n-1 if n+1 not prime? For n=32, p=31 prime: if take first n from p? Need length n from p-1? n=32, p=33 not prime; p=31 has p-1=30, less. Could use p=n+1 only. Good.

`block_start`:
```
def block_start(n, seed):
 rng=np.random.RandomState(seed)
 minL=max(3,n//64); maxL=max(minL+1,n//8)
 L=int(rng.randint(minL, maxL+1)) if minL<maxL else minL
 nb=int((n+L-1)//L)
 vals=rng.randint(0,2,nb)
 h=np.repeat(vals,L)[:n]
 h=np.convolve(h.astype(float), np.ones(3)/3.0, mode='same')
 return top_n_binary(project_feasible(h,n),n)
```

```
import time
import math
import numpy as np
from scipy.optimize import minimize

_stop_time=0.0

def stop_callback(*args):
    return time.time() > _stop_time

def compute_c5(h,n):
    h=np.asarray(h,float)
    dx=2.0/float(n)
    c=np.correlate(h,1.0-h,mode='full')
    return float(np.max(c)*dx)

def recompute_c_int(h,n):
    a=np.asarray(h,dtype=np.int32)
    return np.correlate(a,1-a,mode='full').astype(np.int32)

def project_feasible(a,n): ...
...
```

In `project_feasible`, if `a` has size not n: pad/trunc. If all finite false, return full 0.5? But sum target? Then adjust.
```
def project_feasible(a,n):
    a=np.asarray(a,float).ravel()
    if a.size<n:
       a=np.concatenate([a,np.zeros(n-a.size)])
    elif a.size>n: a=a[:n]
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(a)):
       a=np.nan_to_num(a)
    tgt=0.5*n
    lo=float(np.min(a))-1.0; hi=float(np.max(a))+1.0
    for _ in range(50): ...
    h=np.clip(a-0.5*(lo+hi),0,1)
    for _ in range(30): ...
    diff=tgt-h.sum()
    if diff>0:
      idx=int(np.argmax(1-h)); h[idx]=min(1.0,h[idx]+diff)
    elif diff<0: ...
    return h
```
If `a` empty? n≥2.

def top_n_binary(h,n):
   h=np.asarray(h,float).ravel(); target=n//2
   hb=np.zeros(n)
   if target>0:
     idx=np.argsort(-h)[:target]; hb[idx]=1
   return hb

Let's refine `polish_continuous` to avoid too many variants (candidate_variants inside each beta may cost but okay). We can do variants after each stage to exploit symmetry.
```
def polish_continuous(h,n,betas,pen,deadline,stage_time):
    h=project_feasible(h,n); best=h.copy(); bc=compute_c5(best,n)
    bounds=[(0.0,1.0)]*n
    for beta in betas:
       if time.time() > deadline - 4: break
       _stop_time = min(deadline - 2, time.time() + max(2.0, min(stage_time, 0.5*max(2.0, deadline-time.time()))))
       try:
          res=minimize(lbfgs_objective,h,method='L-BFGS-B',jac=True,bounds=bounds,args=(n,float(beta),float(pen)),callback=stop_callback,options={'maxiter':1000000,'maxls':30,'ftol':1e-13,'gtol':1e-7})
          x=res.x if res is not None and hasattr(res,'x') else h
       except Exception:
          x=h
       h=project_feasible(np.asarray(x,float),n)
       cw=compute_c5(h,n)
       if cw<bc: bc=cw; best=h.copy()
       for cand in candidate_variants(h,n):
           cc=compute_c5(cand,n)
           if cc<bc: bc=cc; best=cand.copy()
       if bc < cw - 1e-12: h=best.copy()
    return best,bc
```
Note `_stop_time` global assignment inside top-level function requires `global _stop_time`? Yes, to assign global variable in function, need `global _stop_time` at top of `polish_continuous`. Or use a list? Better: define global `_stop_time` and in polish `global _stop_time`. Is that allowed? yes top-level. Or use module-level variable and `global`. We'll add.
Alternatively use `stop_callback` reads global; in polish assign `global _stop_time; _stop_time=...`.

```
def binary_search(h0,n,deadline,seed):
    h=top_n_binary(project_feasible(h0,n),n).astype(np.int8)
    c=recompute_c_int(h,n)
    best_h=h.copy(); best_m=int(c.max()); best_E=binary_energy(c)
    zeros=np.nonzero(h==0)[0]; ones=np.nonzero(h==1)[0]
    rng=np.random.RandomState(seed)
    E1=best_E; it=0; T=50.0; stuck=0
    while time.time()<deadline:
       if len(zeros)==0 or len(ones)==0: break
       if it%16==0 or len(zeros)!=n//2: zeros=...; ones=...
       while True:
          i=int(zeros[rng.randint(zeros.size)]); j=int(ones[rng.randint(ones.size)])
          if i!=j and h[i]==0 and h[j]==1: break
          zeros=...; ones=...
          if zeros.size==0... break outer? 
       apply_swap(h,c,i,j,n)
       E2=binary_energy(c)
       accept=False
       if E2 < E1: accept=True
       elif T>0:
          de=E2-E1
          if de < 60*T and rng.random() < math.exp(-de/T): accept=True
       if accept:
          E1=E2
          m=int(c.max())
          if m<best_m or (m==best_m and E1<best_E): best_h=h.copy(); best_m=m; best_E=E1; stuck=0
          else: stuck+=1
       else:
          apply_swap(h,c,j,i,n)
          stuck+=1
       it+=1
       T=max(0.5, T*0.9999)
       if it%2000==0:
          c=recompute_c_int(h,n); E1=binary_energy(c)
          m=int(c.max()); if m<best_m... update
       if stuck>30000 and it>1000:
          h=best_h.copy(); c=recompute_c_int(h,n); E1=best_E; T=20.0; stuck=0
    return best_h.astype(float), float(best_m)*2.0/n
```
Potential issue: If `binary_energy` uses `argpartition` and c negative? no. If c size less than 8? handle.

`binary_energy`:
```
def binary_energy(c):
    m=int(c.max())
    k=8 if c.size>8 else c.size
    if k==c.size:
       s=int(c.sum())
    else:
       s=int(c[np.argpartition(c, -k)[-k:]].sum())
    return m*100000 + s
```
If c size 0? no.

In binary_search, when applying inverse after reject, E1 unchanged. But if due to bug c corrupt, recompute periodically. Good.

So equal top sum accepted. stuck only when accept but no best_E? Could increment often. For equal E but not lower than best_E? best_E is current best; if E1==best_E and accept equal, m same, E1 not < best_E, stuck increments. This could reset too early. Reset to best_h identical if E1==best_E? If current E equals best_E but state different perhaps okay. Reset to best_h loses alternate same energy. Could reset too often. We can increment stuck only when E1 > best_E + 5?

```
def run(seed=42,budget_s=1100,**kwargs):
    t0=time.time()
    try: total=float(budget_s)
    except Exception: total=1100.0
    end=t0+max(30.0, total-12.0)
    try: h0=np.array(initial_h_values,float).copy()
    except Exception: h0=None
    if h0 is None or h0.size<4 or not np.all(np.isfinite(h0)):
       n_base=256; h_base=legendre_h(n_base,seed)
    else:
       n_init=int(h0.size)
       if n_init%2==0 and n_init>=16:
          n_base=n_init; h_base=h0
       else:
          n_base=256; h_base=downsample_h(h0,n_base)
    best_h=project_feasible(h_base,n_base); best_c=compute_c5(best_h,n_base); best_n=n_base
    # cheap variants
    for hv in candidate_variants(best_h,best_n): best_h,best_c,best_n=consider_candidate(hv,best_n,best_h,best_c,best_n)
    # Phase 1 continuous on current
    p1_end = min(end - 350.0, t0 + max(30.0, 0.30*max(30.0,total)))
    if time.time() < p1_end - 30.0:
       ph,pc=polish_continuous(best_h,best_n,[20.0,60.0,200.0,700.0,2500.0],1e4,p1_end,55.0)
       best_h,best_c,best_n=consider_candidate(ph,best_n,best_h,best_c,best_n)
    # Phase 2 binary/continuous per n
    p2_end = min(end - 180.0, t0 + max(60.0, 0.62*max(30.0,total)))
    test_ns=[]
    if best_n not in (256,400):
       test_ns=[256,400]
    else:
       test_ns=[256,400]
    # order maybe 256 first
    for n in [256,400]:
       if n % 2 !=0: continue
       if time.time() > p2_end - 90.0: break
       starts=[]
       if n==256:
          starts.append(legendre_h(256,seed+1))
          starts.append(downsample_h(h_base,n))
          starts.append(block_start(n,seed+7))
       else:
          if h0 is not None and h0.size==n:
             starts.append(h0.copy())
          starts.append(legendre_h(n,seed+2))
          starts.append(block_start(n,seed+13))
       # maybe max 3; allocate remaining
       rem=p2_end-time.time()
       per_start=max(25.0, rem/(2.0*len(starts)))
       for si,hs in enumerate(starts):
          if time.time() > p2_end - 45.0: break
          hb=top_n_binary(project_feasible(hs,n),n)
          best_h,best_c,best_n=consider_candidate(hb,n,best_h,best_c,best_n)
          stage_dl=min(p2_end-20.0, time.time()+per_start*0.6)
          if time.time() < stage_dl - 5.0:
             bh,bc=binary_search(hb,n,stage_dl,seed+n*100+si)
             best_h,best_c,best_n=consider_candidate(bh,n,best_h,best_c,best_n)
          if time.time() < p2_end - 30.0:
             ph,pc=polish_continuous(best_h if best_n==n else downsample_h(best_h,n), n, [150.0,800.0],1e4, min(p2_end-5.0, time.time()+per_start*0.4), 18.0)
             best_h,best_c,best_n=consider_candidate(ph,n,best_h,best_c,best_n)
    # Phase 3 final
    for hv in candidate_variants(best_h,best_n): update
    if best_n%2==0 and time.time() < end-60:
       hb=top_n_binary(best_h,best_n)
       best=consider...
       stage_dy = min(end-35, time.time()+40)
       bh,bc=binary_search(hb,best_n,stage_dy,seed+999)
       update
    if time.time() < end-80:
       ph,pc=polish_continuous(best_h,best_n,[1000.0,5000.0,20000.0],1e4,end-45,25.0)
       update
    if time.time() < end-45:
       for scale in (0.004,0.012):
          if time.time() > end-25: break
          nh=project_feasible(best_h + scale*np.random.RandomState(seed+len([..]))? Need rng create; use np.random.RandomState(seed+int(scale*1000))
          ph,pc=polish_continuous(nh,best_n,[8000.0],1e4,end-15,15)
          update
    # final small n
    if time.time() < end-40:
       for nnew in (320,256):
          if time.time() > end-15: break
          hr=project_feasible(downsample_h(best_h,nnew),nnew)
          ph,pc=polish_continuous(hr,nnew,[500.0,3000.0],1e4,end-10,12)
          update
    # final variants and binary top
    for hv in candidate_variants(best_h,best_n): update
    if best_n%2==0:
       tb=top_n_binary(best_h,best_n); consider
    # ensure feasible and compute final
    best_h=project_feasible(best_h,best_n)
    best_c=compute_c5(best_h,best_n)
    return best_h,best_c,int(best_n)
```

Let's write `downsample_h`:
```
def downsample_h(h,n):
    h=np.asarray(h,float).ravel()
    if h.size==n: return h
    if h.size<2: return np.full(n,0.5)
    xo=np.linspace(0.0,2.0,h.size,endpoint=False)
    xn=np.linspace(0.0,2.0,n,endpoint=False)
    return np.interp(xn,xo,h)
```

```
_idx_cache = {}
def grad_indices(n):
   if n not in _idx_cache:
      i=np.arange(n)[:,None]; j=np.arange(n)[None,:]
      ctr=n-1
      idxA=ctr + (i - j)
      idxB=ctr - (i - j)
      _idx_cache[n]=(idxA.astype(np.int64), idxB.astype(np.int64))
   return _idx_cache[n]
```

```python
import time
import math
import numpy as np
from scipy.optimize import minimize

_stop_time = 0.0
_idx_cache = {}

def stop_callback(*args):
    return time.time() > _stop_time

def compute_c5(h, n):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float).ravel()
    dx = 2.0 / float(n)
    c = np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode="full")
    return float(np.max(c) * dx)

def recompute_c_int(h, n):
    a = np.asarray(h, dtype=np.int32).ravel()
    return np.correlate(a, 1 - a, mode="full").astype(np.int32)

def project_feasible(a, n):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=float).ravel()
    if a.size < n:
        a = np.concatenate([a, np.zeros(max(0, n - a.size))])
    elif a.size > n:
        a = a[:n]
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(a)):
        a = np.nan_to_num(a, nan=0.5, posinf=1.0, neginf=0.0)
    tgt = 0.5 * float(n)
    lo = float(np.min(a)) - 1.0
    hi = float(np.max(a)) + 1.0
    for _ in range(55):
        mid = 0.5 * (lo + hi)
        s = float(np.clip(a - mid, 0.0, 1.0).sum())
        if s > tgt:
            lo = mid
        else:
            hi = mid
    h = np.clip(a - 0.5 * (lo + hi), 0.0, 1.0)
    for _ in range(30):
        diff = tgt - float(h.sum())
        if abs(diff) < 1e-13:
            break
        if diff > 0.0:
            room = 1.0 - h
            s = float(room.sum())
            if s <= 1e-15:
                break
            h += diff * room / s
        else:
            s = float(h.sum())
            if s <= 1e-15:
                break
            h += diff * h / s
    h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)
    diff = tgt - float(h.sum())
    if abs(diff) > 1e-14:
        if diff > 0.0:
            idx = int(np.argmax(1.0 - h))
            h[idx] = min(1.0, h[idx] + diff)
        else:
            idx = int(np.argmax(h))
            h[idx] = max(0.0, h[idx] + diff)
    return h

def top_n_binary(h, n):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float).ravel()
    if h.size != n:
        h = project_feasible(h, n)
    tgt = n // 2
    hb = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    if tgt > 0:
        idx = np.argsort(-h)[:tgt]
        hb[idx] = 1.0
    return hb

def downsample_h(h, n):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float).ravel()
    if h.size == n:
        return h.copy()
    if h.size < 2:
        return np.full(n, 0.5, dtype=float)
    xo = np.linspace(0.0, 2.0, h.size, endpoint=False)
    xn = np.linspace(0.0, 2.0, n, endpoint=False)
    return np.interp(xn, xo, h)

def is_prime_small(p):
    if p < 2:
        return False
    if p % 2 == 0:
        return p == 2
    d = 3
    while d * d <= p:
        if p % d == 0:
            return False
        d += 2
    return True

def legendre_h(n, seed=0):
    if n > 1 and (n + 1) % 2 == 0:
        pass
    p = n + 1
    if n > 1 and (n % 2 == 0) and is_prime_small(p):
        e = (p - 1) // 2
        sym = np.array([pow(r, e, p) for r in range(1, n + 1)], dtype=int)
        return np.where(sym == 1, 1.0, 0.0)
    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed + 911)
    h = rng.rand(n)
    k = max(2, n // 24)
    h = np.convolve(h, np.ones(k) / float(k), mode="same")
    return project_feasible(h, n)

def block_start(n, seed):
    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)
    minL = max(3, n // 64)
    maxL = max(minL + 1, n // 8)
    L = int(rng.randint(minL, maxL + 1))
    nb = (n + L - 1) // L
    vals = rng.randint(0, 2, nb).astype(float)
    h = np.repeat(vals, L)[:n].astype(float)
    h = np.convolve(h, np.ones(5) / 5.0, mode="same")
    return top_n_binary(project_feasible(h, n), n)

def candidate_variants(h, n):
    h = project_feasible(h, n)
    out = [
        h,
        h[::-1],
        1.0 - h[::-1],
        0.5 * (h + h[::-1]),
        0.5 * (h + (1.0 - h[::-1])),
    ]
    if n % 2 == 0:
        out.append(top_n_binary(h, n))
    res = []
    for x in out:
        res.append(project_feasible(x, n))
    return res

def grad_indices(n):
    if n in _idx_cache:
        return _idx_cache[n]
    i = np.arange(n)[:, None]
    j = np.arange(n)[None, :]
    ctr = n - 1
    d = i - j
    idxA = (ctr + d).astype(np.int64)
    idxB = (ctr - d).astype(np.int64)
    _idx_cache[n] = (idxA, idxB)
    return idxA, idxB

def lse_objective_grad(h, n, beta, pen):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float).ravel()
    if h.size != n:
        h = project_feasible(h, n)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(h)):
        h = np.nan_to_num(h, nan=0.5, posinf=1.0, neginf=0.0)
    h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)
    dx = 2.0 / float(n)
    v = 1.0 - h
    c = np.correlate(h, v, mode="full") * dx
    c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)
    m = float(c.max())
    y = float(beta) * (c - m)
    e = np.exp(np.minimum(y, 0.0))
    s = float(e.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        P = np.full(c.shape, 1.0 / float(c.size))
        F = m
    else:
        P = e / s
        F = m + math.log(s) / float(beta)
    idxA, idxB = grad_indices(n)
    A = P[idxA].dot(v)
    B = P[idxB].dot(h)
    g = dx * (A - B)
    dev = float(h.sum() - 0.5 * n)
    if pen != 0.0:
        F += 0.5 * float(pen) * dev * dev
        g = g + float(pen) * dev
    if not np.isfinite(F):
        return 1e100, np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    return float(F), g

def lbfgs_objective(x, n, beta, pen):
    return lse_objective_grad(x, n, beta, pen)

def polish_continuous(h, n, betas, pen, deadline, stage_time):
    global _stop_time
    h = project_feasible(h, n)
    best = h.copy()
    bc = compute_c5(best, n)
    bounds = [(0.0, 1.0)] * n
    for beta in betas:
        if time.time() > deadline - 4.0:
            break
        remaining = max(2.0, deadline - time.time())
        _stop_time = min(deadline - 2.0, time.time() + max(2.0, min(float(stage_time), 0.5 * remaining)))
        try:
            res = minimize(
                lbfgs_objective, h, method="L-BFGS-B", jac=True,
                bounds=bounds, args=(int(n), float(beta), float(pen)),
                callback=stop_callback,
                options={"maxiter": 1000000, "maxls": 30, "ftol": 1e-13, "gtol": 1e-7}
            )
            x = res.x if (res is not None and hasattr(res, "x")) else h
        except Exception:
            x = h
        h = project_feasible(np.asarray(x, dtype=float), n)
        cw = compute_c5(h, n)
        if cw < bc:
            bc = cw
            best = h.copy()
        for cand in candidate_variants(h, n):
            cc = compute_c5(cand, n)
            if cc < bc:
                bc = cc
                best = cand.copy()
        if bc < cw - 1e-12:
            h = best.copy()
    return best, bc

def apply_swap(h, c, i, j, n):
    ctr = n - 1
    if i + 1 < n:
        L = n - 1 - i
        c[ctr + 1:ctr + 1 + L] -= h[i + 1:]
        c[ctr - L:ctr] += 1 - h[i + 1:][::-1]
    if i > 0:
        c[ctr + 1:ctr + 1 + i] += 1 - h[i - 1::-1]
        c[ctr - i:ctr] -= h[:i]
    if j + 1 < n:
        L = n - 1 - j
        c[ctr + 1:ctr + 1 + L] += h[j + 1:]
        c[ctr - L:ctr] -= 1 - h[j + 1:][::-1]
    if j > 0:
        c[ctr + 1:ctr + 1 + j] -= 1 - h[j - 1::-1]
        c[ctr - j:ctr] += h[:j]
    c[ctr + i - j] += 1
    c[ctr + j - i] += 1
    h[i] = 1
    h[j] = 0

def binary_energy(c):
    m = int(c.max())
    k = 8 if c.size > 8 else c.size
    if k == c.size:
        s = int(c.sum())
    else:
        s = int(c[np.argpartition(c, -k)[-k:]].sum())
    return m * 100000 + s

def binary_search(h0, n, deadline, seed):
    h = top_n_binary(project_feasible(h0, n), n).astype(np.int8)
    c = recompute_c_int(h, n)
    best_h = h.copy()
    best_m = int(c.max())
    best_E = binary_energy(c)
    zeros = np.nonzero(h == 0)[0]
    ones = np.nonzero(h == 1)[0]
    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)
    E1 = best_E
    it = 0
    T = 50.0
    stuck = 0
    while time.time() < deadline:
        if zeros.size == 0 or ones.size == 0:
            break
        if it % 16 == 0:
            zeros = np.nonzero(h == 0)[0]
            ones = np.nonzero(h == 1)[0]
            if zeros.size == 0 or ones.size == 0:
                break
        i = int(zeros[rng.randint(zeros.size)])
        j = int(ones[rng.randint(ones.size)])
        if h[i] != 0 or h[j] != 1 or i == j:
            zeros = np.nonzero(h == 0)[0]
            ones = np.nonzero(h == 1)[0]
            if zeros.size == 0 or ones.size == 0:
                break
            continue
        apply_swap(h, c, i, j, n)
        E2 = binary_energy(c)
        accept = False
        if E2 < E1:
            accept = True
        elif T > 0.0:
            de = float(E2 - E1)
            if de < 60.0 * T and rng.random() < math.exp(-de / T):
                accept = True
        if accept:
            E1 = E2
            m = int(c.max())
            if m < best_m or (m == best_m and E1 < best_E):
                best_h = h.copy()
                best_m = m
                best_E = E1
                stuck = 0
            else:
                if E1 <= best_E + 8:
                    stuck = 0
                else:
                    stuck += 1
        else:
            apply_swap(h, c, j, i, n)
            stuck += 1
        it += 1
        T = max(0.5, T * 0.9999)
        if it % 2000 == 0:
            c = recompute_c_int(h, n)
            E1 = binary_energy(c)
            m = int(c.max())
            if m < best_m or (m == best_m and E1 < best_E):
                best_h = h.copy()
                best_m = m
                best_E = E1
        if stuck > 30000 and it > 1000:
            h = best_h.copy()
            c = recompute_c_int(h, n)
            E1 = best_E
            T = 20.0
            stuck = 0
    return best_h.astype(float), float(best_m) * (2.0 / float(n))

def consider_candidate(h, n, best_h, best_c, best_n):
    try:
        hh = project_feasible(h, int(n))
        nn = int(n)
        cc = compute_c5(hh, nn)
        if cc < best_c - 1e-12:
            return hh, cc, nn
    except Exception:
        pass
    return best_h, best_c, best_n

def run(seed=42, budget_s=1100, **kwargs):
    t0 = time.time()
    try:
        total = float(budget_s)
    except Exception:
        total = 1100.0
    end = t0 + max(30.0, total - 12.0)

    try:
        h0 = np.array(initial_h_values, dtype=float).copy()
    except Exception:
        h0 = None
    if h0 is None or h0.size < 4 or not np.all(np.isfinite(h0)):
        n_base = 256
        h_base = legendre_h(n_base, seed)
    else:
        n_init = int(h0.size)
        if n_init % 2 == 0 and n_init >= 16:
            n_base = n_init
            h_base = h0
        else:
            n_base = 256
            h_base = downsample_h(h0, n_base)

    best_h = project_feasible(h_base, n_base)
    best_c = compute_c5(best_h, n_base)
    best_n = n_base

    for hv in candidate_variants(best_h, best_n):
        best_h, best_c, best_n = consider_candidate(hv, best_n, best_h, best_c, best_n)

    p1_end = min(end - 340.0, t0 + max(30.0, 0.30 * max(30.0, total)))
    if time.time() < p1_end - 30.0:
        ph, pc = polish_continuous(
            best_h, best_n, [20.0, 60.0, 200.0, 700.0, 2500.0],
            1e4, p1_end, 55.0
        )
        best_h, best_c, best_n = consider_candidate(ph, best_n, best_h, best_c, best_n)

    p2_end = min(end - 170.0, t0 + max(60.0, 0.62 * max(30.0, total)))
    for n in [256, 400]:
        if n % 2 != 0:
            continue
        if time.time() > p2_end - 85.0:
            break
        starts = []
        if n == 256:
            starts.append(legendre_h(n, seed + 1))
            starts.append(downsample_h(h_base, n))
            starts.append(block_start(n, seed + 7))
        else:
            if h0 is not None and h0.size == n:
                starts.append(h0.copy())
            starts.append(legendre_h(n, seed + 2))
            starts.append(block_start(n, seed + 13))

        rem = max(10.0, p2_end - time.time())
        per_start = max(25.0, rem / (2.0 * max(1, len(starts))))
        for si, hs in enumerate(starts):
            if time.time() > p2_end - 40.0:
                break
            hb = top_n_binary(project_feasible(hs, n), n)
            best_h, best_c, best_n = consider_candidate(hb, n, best_h, best_c, best_n)

            stage_dl = min(p2_end - 18.0, time.time() + per_start * 0.60)
            if time.time() < stage_dl - 5.0:
                bh, bc = binary_search(hb, n, stage_dl, seed + 1000 * n + si)
                best_h, best_c, best_n = consider_candidate(bh, n, best_h, best_c, best_n)

            if time.time() < p2_end - 25.0:
                src = best_h if best_n == n else downsample_h(best_h, n)
                ph, pc = polish_continuous(
                    src, n, [150.0, 800.0], 1e4,
                    min(p2_end - 5.0, time.time() + per_start * 0.40), 18.0
                )
                best_h, best_c, best_n = consider_candidate(ph, n, best_h, best_c, best_n)

    for hv in candidate_variants(best_h, best_n):
        best_h, best_c, best_n = consider_candidate(hv, best_n, best_h, best_c, best_n)

    if best_n % 2 == 0 and time.time() < end - 70.0:
        hb = top_n_binary(best_h, best_n)
        best_h, best_c, best_n = consider_candidate(hb, best_n, best_h, best_c, best_n)
        stage_dl = min(end - 40.0, time.time() + 35.0)
        if time.time() < stage_dl - 5.0:
            bh, bc = binary_search(hb, best_n, stage_dl, seed + 9993)
            best_h, best_c, best_n = consider_candidate(bh, best_n, best_h, best_c, best_n)

    if time.time() < end - 80.0:
        ph, pc = polish_continuous(
            best_h, best_n, [1000.0, 5000.0, 20000.0], 1e4, end - 40.0, 25.0
        )
        best_h, best_c, best_n = consider_candidate(ph, best_n, best_h, best_c, best_n)

    rng_extra = np.random.RandomState(seed + 777777)
    if time.time() < end - 45.0:
        for scale in (0.004, 0.012):
            if time.time() > end - 20.0:
                break
            nh = project_feasible(best_h + scale * rng_extra.randn(best_n), best_n)
            ph, pc = polish_continuous(nh, best_n, [8000.0], 1e4, end - 10.0, 12.0)
            best_h, best_c, best_n = consider_candidate(ph, best_n, best_h, best_c, best_n)

    if time.time() < end - 40.0:
        for nnew in (320, 256):
            if time.time() > end - 12.0:
                break
            hr = project_feasible(downsample_h(best_h, nnew), nnew)
            ph, pc = polish_continuous(hr, nnew, [500.0, 3000.0], 1e4, end - 8.0, 12.0)
            best_h, best_c, best_n = consider_candidate(ph, nnew, best_h, best_c, best_n)

    for hv in candidate_variants(best_h, best_n):
        best_h, best_c, best_n = consider_candidate(hv, best_n, best_h, best_c, best_n)

    if best_n % 2 == 0:
        tb = top_n_binary(best_h, best_n)
        best_h, best_c, best_n = consider_candidate(tb, best_n, best_h, best_c, best_n)

    best_h = project_feasible(best_h, best_n)
    best_c = compute_c5(best_h, best_n)
    return best_h, best_c, int(best_n)
```
